\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{2m+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2m-2}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{2m+2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}},

we obtain

(7.49)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{2m+1}}(z)}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=1}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2m-p+4s+2t-1})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

On the other hand, the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{4n+p-3}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{p-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}}

implies that

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{2m+1}}(z)d_{n,n^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-4n-p+3}z)}{d_{n^{p-1},n^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}\times\dfrac{a_{n^{p-1},n^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}{a_{n^{p},n^{2m+1}}(z)a_{n,n^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-4n-p+3}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By direct computation, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{n^{p-1},n^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}{a_{n^{p},n^{2m+1}}(z)a_{n,n^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-4n-p+3}z)}=\prod_{s=1}^{n}\dfrac{(z-(-1)^{p+1}(q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{4n-2m+p-1-4s})}{(z-(-1)^{p+1}(q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2m+p-3+4s})}

and

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{2m+1}}(z)d_{n,n^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-4n-p+3}z)}{d_{n^{p-1},n^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}=\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{2m+1}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{4n+4s+2m+p-5})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-2}\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2m-p+4s+2t-1})},

which follows from the descending induction.
Thus we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{2m+1}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{4n+4s+2m+p-5})(z-(-1)^{p+1}(q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{4n-2m+p-1-4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2m-p+4s+2t-1})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}]

(7.50)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{2m+1}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{p+1}(q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{4n-2m+p-1-4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2m-p+4s+2t-1})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Thus our assertion hold for odd
p
, since

2m-p+4s^{\prime}-1\neq 4n-2m+p-1-4s

by comparing (
7.49
) and (
7.50
).
∎

7.3.
Proof for type
C_{n}^{(1)}

In this subsection, we first prove our assertion on
d_{k^{m},n^{p}}(z)
for
1\leq k<n
and on
d_{n^{m},n^{p}}(z)
.
Then we will prove our assertion on
d_{k^{m},l^{p}}(z)
for
1\leq k,l<n
, by applying the similar argument in Section
7.1
.

Recall, for
1\leq k,l\leq n
, we have

(7.51)

\displaystyle

\displaystyle d_{k,l}(z)=\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{\min(k,l,n-k,n-l)}\big(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{|k-l|+2s}\big)\prod_{i=1}^{\min(k,l)}\big(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2-k-l+2s}\big),

\displaystyle a_{k,l}(z)\equiv\dfrac{{}_{s}\hskip-2.15277pt\left[|k-l|\right]{}_{s}\hskip-2.15277pt\left[2n+2-k-l\right]{}_{s}\hskip-2.15277pt\left[2n+2+k+l\right]{}_{s}\hskip-2.15277pt\left[4n+4-|k-l|\right]}{{}_{s}\hskip-2.15277pt\left[k+l\right]{}_{s}\hskip-2.15277pt\left[2n+2-k+l\right]{}_{s}\hskip-2.15277pt\left[2n+2+k-l\right]{}_{s}\hskip-2.15277pt\left[4n+4-k-l\right]},

Note also that we have surjective homomorphisms

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k-1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{1-l}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l-1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{-q_{\mathfrak{s}}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)

for all
1<k,l\leq n
, as Dorey’s rule in Theorem
3.15
, and surjective homomorphisms

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1-n+k}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+1-k}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)

given in (
3.13b
) for
1\leq k<n
.

7.3.1.
d_{l^{m},n^{p}}(z)
for
1\leq l<n

As Lemma
7.12
, we can prove the following lemma:

Lemma 7.35
.

We have

d_{1,n^{2}}(z)=(z+(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+5}).

Proof.

By (
7.51
), we have
d_{1,n}(z)=\big(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+3}\big)
.
Hence it is enough to consider

\displaystyle{\rm(i)}

\displaystyle\ \ \mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+5}})=\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+3}}\mathbin{\scalebox{.9}{$\nabla$}}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+7}})

\displaystyle{\rm(ii)}

\displaystyle\ \ \mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+1}})=\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n-1}}\mathbin{\scalebox{.9}{$\nabla$}}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+3}})

(i) Note that the sequences of root modules

(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+3}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+7}})\quad\text{{and}}\quad(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+3}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+7}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big))

are normal, since

\mathfrak{d}(\mathscr{D}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+7}})=0

and
\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+7}})=0
, respectively. Hence Lemma
2.17
(
b
) (v) says that

\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+3}}\mathbin{\scalebox{.9}{$\nabla$}}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+7}})=\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+3}})+\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+7}})=1.

(ii) Note that

\mathrm{rch}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big))=\boldsymbol{[}{0}\boldsymbol{]}\quad\text{{and}}\quad\mathrm{exrch}_{1}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+1}})=\boldsymbol{[}{-2,2n+4}\boldsymbol{]}.

Hence Theorem
3.12
implies

\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+1}})=0,

which completes our assertion.
∎

With Lemma
7.35
, we can prove the following lemma.

Lemma 7.36
.

For
m\geq 1
and
1\leq l<n
, we have

d_{l,n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{(n+m+l+1)}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+2m+2s}).

Lemma 7.37
.

For
p\geq 1
and
1\leq l<n
, we have

(7.52)

d_{l^{p},n}(z)=\prod_{t=0}^{\min(p,2)-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{(n+p+l+1)}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+|2-p|+2s+2t}).

Proof.

Our assertion is known for
p=1
. Therefore, we assume
p\geq 2
.
By fusion rule,

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l,n}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)d_{l,n}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}z)}{d_{l^{2},n}(z)}=\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{n+l}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+2s+2t}).}{d_{l^{2},n}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

On the other hand, the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+3}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n-1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})}

implies that we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{2},n}(z)(z-(-1)^{n+l}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+1})(z-(-1)^{n+l}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{3n-l+7})}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{n+l}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+1+2s})(z-(-1)^{n+l}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+3+2s})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}],

which implies our assertion when
p=2
.
For
p\geq 3
, we can apply the similar argument.
∎

As Lemma
7.6
and Lemma
7.17
, we have the following analogous statement by splitting it into the cases when
p\leq 2m
and
p>2m
.

Lemma 7.38
.

For
m,p\geq 1
and
1\leq l<n
, we have

d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)\text{ divides}\prod_{t=0}^{\min(p,2m)-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{n+p+l+m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+|2m-p|+2s+2t}).

Proof.

(a) Let us assume that
p\leq 2m
.
By fusion rule

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{1-p}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

we have

\dfrac{d_{l^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)d_{l,n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{1-p}z)}{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By induction,

d_{l^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)\text{ divides }\prod_{t=0}^{p-2}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{n+p+l+m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+2m-p+2s+2t})

and we know

d_{l,n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{1-p}z)=\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{n+m+l+1}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+2m+p-1+2s}).

Thus our assertion follows. The remaining case can be proved in a similar way.
∎

Lemma 7.39
.

For
m,p\geq 1
with
|2m-p|\geq l-1
and
1\leq l<n
, set
d=n+p+l+m
. Then
we have

\displaystyle d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{t=0}^{\min(p,2m)-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+|2m-p|+2s+2t}).

Proof.

Note that we have proved when
\min(m,p)=1
. Thus we will consider when
\min(m,p)>1
.

(a) Let us assume that
p\leq 2m
.
By the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+p+1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}},

we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{l,n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n-p-1}z)}{d_{l^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}\times\dfrac{a_{l^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}{a_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{l,n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n-p-1}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By the induction on
p
, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{l,n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n-p-1}z)}{d_{l^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}=\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{3n-l+2m+p+1+2s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-2}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+2m-p+2s+2t})}

and, direct computation yields

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{l^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}{a_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{l,n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n-p-1}z)}=\prod_{s=1}^{l}\dfrac{(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+p-1-2m+2s})}{(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+p-1+2m+2s})}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{3n-l+2m+p+1+2s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+2m-p+2s+2t})}\times\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+p-1-2m+2s})\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Since

\displaystyle 3n-l+2m+p+1+2s^{\prime}\neq n+1-l+2m-p+2s+2t\iff 2n+2p

\displaystyle\neq 2(s-s^{\prime})+2t

for
1\leq s,s^{\prime}\leq l
and
0\leq t\leq p-1
, the above implies

(7.53)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+p-1-2m+2s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+2m-p+1+2s+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}],

which proves our assertion under the assumption with Lemma
7.38
, since
(u\mathbin{:=}2m-p\geq l-1)

n-l-1-u+2s^{\prime}\neq n-l+u+1+2s+2t\iff 2(s^{\prime}-s)-2t\neq 2u+2

for
1\leq s,s^{\prime}\leq l
and
0\leq t\leq p-1
.

(b) Assume that
2m<p
. By the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-1)^{1-m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2n+2m}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-1}},

we have by the induction on
m

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{l^{p},n}((-1)^{1-m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-2n-2m}z)}{d_{l^{p},n^{m-1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})z)}\times\dfrac{a_{l^{p},n^{m-1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})z)}{a_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{l^{p},n}((-1)^{1-m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-2n-2m}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By the descending induction on
p-2m
, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{l^{p},n}((-1)^{1-m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-2n-2m}z)}{d_{l^{p},n^{m-1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})z)}=\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{n+p+l+m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{3n+1-l+p+2m-2+2s+2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{2m-3}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{n+p+l+m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+p-2m+2s+2t})}

and the direct computation yields

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{l^{p},n^{m-1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})z)}{a_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{l^{p},n}((-1)^{1-m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-2n-2m}z)}=\prod_{t=0}^{1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}\dfrac{(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+l-p+2m+1-2s-2t})}{(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+p+2m-3+2s+2t})}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{3n+1-l+p+2m-2+2s+2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{2m-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+p-2m+2s+2t})}\times\prod_{t=0}^{1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+l-p+2m+1-2s-2t})\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Since

\displaystyle 3n+1-l+p+2m-2+2s+2t\neq n+1-l+p-2m+2s^{\prime}+2t^{\prime}

\displaystyle\iff

\displaystyle 2n+4m\neq 2(s^{\prime}-s)+2(t^{\prime}-t)+2

for
1\leq s,s^{\prime}\leq l
,
0\leq t^{\prime}\leq 2m-1
and
0\leq t\leq p-1
, we have

(7.54)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+l-p+2m+1-2s-2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{2m-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+p-2m+2s+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}],

which implies our assertion under our assumption with Lemma
7.38
, since
(u\mathbin{:=}p-2m\geq l-1)

n+l-p+2m+1-2s-2t\neq n+1-l+p-2m+2s^{\prime}+2t^{\prime}\iff 2l-2(s+s^{\prime})-2(t+t^{\prime})\neq 2u,

for
1\leq s,s^{\prime}\leq l
,
0\leq t\leq 1
and
0\leq t^{\prime}\leq 2m-1
.
∎

Proof of
Theorem
6.5
for
1\leq l<n=k
in type
C_{n}^{(1)}
.

We first assume that
p\leq 2m
.
From the homomorphism in obtained from Theorem
5.12
(
5.10a
) with the restriction
\min(k,l)=1

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-l+3}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{(l-1)^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})},

we obtain

\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{1^{p},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-l+3}z)}{d_{(l-1)^{p},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}\times\dfrac{a_{(l-1)^{p},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}{a_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{1^{p},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-l+3}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By direct computation, (set
d=n+p+l+m
)

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{(l-1)^{p},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}{a_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{1^{p},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-l+3}z)}=\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\dfrac{(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-n+l-2m+p-3-2t})}{(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{l-n+2m-p-1+2t})}.

Furthermore, by an induction on
l
, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{1^{p},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-l+3}z)}{d_{(l-1)^{p},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}=\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-n+2m-p+l-1+2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l-1}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+2m-p+2s+2t})}

Thus we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-n+l-2m+p-3-2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l-1}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+2m-p+2s+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}]

(7.55)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l-1}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+2m-p+2s+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}],

by induction on
l
. Here we use

-n+l-2m+p-3-2t^{\prime}\neq n+1-l+2m-p+2s+2t\iff l\neq n+2+(2m-p)+s+(t+t^{\prime})

for
1\leq s\leq l-1
and
0\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq p-1
.

On the other hand, fusion rule

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-m-1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-1}}

we have

(7.56)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=2}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+2m-p+1+2s+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}],

which follows from the descending induction on
2m-p
.
Then we have

n-l+2m-p+1+2s+2t^{\prime}\neq n+1+l+2m-p+2t\quad\text{ for }0\leq t^{\prime}\leq 1,\ 1\leq s\leq l,\ 0\leq t\leq p-1,

unless
(t^{\prime},t,s)=(0,0,l)
,
(1,0,l-1)
or
(1,1,l)
when we compare (
7.55
) and (
7.56
).
Similarly,

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{m+1}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})}

yields

\dfrac{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n-l+p-2m-3+2s+2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-l+2m-p+2s+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}]

which completes our assertion for this case,

The remaining case is proved similarly.
∎

7.3.2.
d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)

Lemma 7.40
.

For
m\geq 1
, we have

d_{n,n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+1}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2+|2m-2|+2s}).

Proof.

The case for
m=1
is already known. Thus, we assume
m\geq 2
.

By Dorey’s rule in Theorem
3.15
,

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{1-n}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n-1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

we have

\dfrac{d_{1,n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{1-n}z)d_{n-1,n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}{d_{n,n^{m}}(z)}=\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+1}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2m+2s})}{d_{n,n^{m}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Next the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+3}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n-1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})}

implies that we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n,n^{m}}(z)(z-(-1)^{m+1}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2n+2m+4})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2-2m})}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+1}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2+2m+2s})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Hence our claim follows.
∎

Following the proof of, e.g., Lemma
7.6
, we have the following analogous statement.

Lemma 7.41
.

For
m,p\geq 1
, we have

d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)
divides
\prod_{t=0}^{\min(p,m)-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2+|2m-2p|+2s+4t}).

Lemma 7.42
.

For
|m-p|\geq\lceil n/2\rceil
, we have

\displaystyle d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{t=0}^{\min(p,m)-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2+|2m-2p|+2s+4t}).

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we assume that
p\leq m
. The case when
\min(m,p)=1
was proven above, hence let us consider when
\min(m,p)\geq 2
.
By the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-1)^{1-m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2n+2p}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{p-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-1}},

we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{n,n^{m}}((-1)^{1-m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-2n-2p}z)}{d_{n^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2}z)}\times\dfrac{a_{n^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2}z)}{a_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{n,n^{m}}((-1)^{1-m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-2n-2p}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By descending induction on
m-p
, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{n,n^{m}}((-1)^{1-m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-2n-2p}z)}{d_{n^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})z)}=\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2n+2m+2p+2s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-2}\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2+2m-2p+2s+4t})}

and the direct computation yields

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{n^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})z)}{a_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{n,n^{m}}((-1)^{1-m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-2n-2p}z)}=\prod_{s=1}^{n}\dfrac{(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-2m+2p-2+2s})}{(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2m+2p-2+2s})}

Thus we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2n+2m+2p+2s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2+2m-2p+2s+4t})}\times\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-2m+2p-2+2s})\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}],

which implies

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-2m+2p-2+2s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{d}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2+2m-2p+2s+4t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Here we used

\displaystyle 2n+2m+2p+2s^{\prime}\neq 2+2m-2p+2s+4t\iff n+2p-1\neq(s-s^{\prime})+2t

for
1\leq s\leq n
and
0\leq t\leq p-1
.
Thus our assertion under the assumption follows, since

\displaystyle-2m+2p-2+2s^{\prime}\neq 2+2m-2p+2s+4t\iff(s^{\prime}-s)-2t\neq 2+2u\quad(u\mathbin{:=}m-p\geq\lceil n/2\rceil)

for
1\leq s,s^{\prime}\leq n
and
0\leq t\leq p-1
.
∎

Proof of
Theorem
6.5
for
l=k=n
in type
C_{n}^{(1)}
.

We assume without loss of generality that
p\leq m
.
From the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-m-1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-1}}

and the descending induction on
\left\lvert m-p\right\rvert
, we obtain

\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{n^{p},n}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-m-1}z)}{d_{n^{p},n^{m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-1}z)}\times\dfrac{a_{n^{p},n^{m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-1}z)}{a_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{n^{p},n}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-m-1}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By direct computation, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{n^{p},n^{m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-1}z)}{a_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{n^{p},n}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-m-1}z)}

\displaystyle=1

and

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{n^{p},n}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-m-1}z)}{d_{n^{p},n^{m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2})^{-1}z)}=\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{p+m}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2p+2m+2+2s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2m+6-2p+2s+4t})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=1}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2m+2-2p+2s+4t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Thus we conclude

d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{t=1}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2+2m-2p+2s+4t})\times\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2+2m-2p+2s})^{\epsilon_{s}}

for some
\epsilon_{s}\in\{0,1\}
.

From the homomorphism obtained from Theorem
5.12
(
5.10d
)
without
the restriction on
l

\displaystyle\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{2m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-1)^{-n-m+1}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-n-2}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2}},

we have

\dfrac{d_{n^{p},1^{2m}}((-1)^{-n-m+1}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-n-2}z)d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)}{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2}z)}\times\dfrac{a_{n^{p},n^{m}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2}z)}{a_{n^{p},1^{2m}}((-1)^{-n-m+1}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-n-2}z)a_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By direct calculation,
we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2+2m-2p+2s})^{\epsilon_{s}}}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{4+2m-2p+2s})^{\epsilon_{s}}}\times\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2n+2m-2p+6+4t})(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2p-2m-4t})

\displaystyle\hskip 64.58313pt\times\dfrac{(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2n+2m-2p+4})}{(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2m-2p+4})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}]

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2+2m-2p+2s})^{\epsilon_{s}}}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n}(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{4+2m-2p+2s})^{\epsilon_{s}}}\times\dfrac{(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2n+2m-2p+4})}{(z-(-1)^{m+p}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{2m-2p+4})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Thus
\epsilon_{1}
must be
1
and hence
\epsilon_{k}=1
for all
1\leq s\leq n
, which implies our assertion.
∎

7.3.3.
d_{k^{m},l^{p}}(z)
for
1\leq k,l<n

Lemma 7.43
.

For
m\geq 1
, we have

d_{1,1^{m}}(z)=(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m+1})(z-(-q)^{2n+m+1}).

Proof.

By fusion rules

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{1-m}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m-1}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

we have by induction on
m

\displaystyle\dfrac{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m-1})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m-1})\times(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m+1})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m+1})}{d_{1,1^{m}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}],

\displaystyle\dfrac{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-m+3})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-m+3})\times(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m+1})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m+1})}{d_{1,1^{m}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Therefore, there is an ambiguity when at
m=2
or
m=n+2
.
For the case of
m=2
, the surjective morphism (
3.13b
)

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-n}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

which resolves the ambiguity since

\dfrac{d_{1,n}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-n}z)d_{1,n}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n}z)}{d_{1,k^{2}}(z)}=\dfrac{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+3})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{3})}{d_{1,k^{2}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

For the case of
m=n+2
, fusion rule

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{n}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{n+2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)

resolves the ambiguity since it implies

\displaystyle\dfrac{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{3-n})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+3})\times(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n+3})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{3n+3})}{d_{1,1^{n+2}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

On the other hand, the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m+1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}}

implies that we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{1,1^{m}}(z)(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m+3})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{4n+m+3})}{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m+1})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m+1})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}],

from which our assertion follows.
∎

With Lemma
7.43
, one can check the following lemma.

Lemma 7.44
.

For
1\leq k\leq n-1
and
m\geq 1
, we have

d_{k,1^{m}}=(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m+k})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m-k+2}).

Lemma 7.45
.

For
1\leq k\leq n-1
and
m\geq 1
, we have

d_{1,k^{m}}=(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m+k})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m-k+2}).

Proof.

By fusion rules

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{1-m}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m-1}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

we have

(7.57a)

\displaystyle\dfrac{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m+k-2})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m-k})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m+k})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m-k+2})}{d_{1,k^{m}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}],

(7.57b)

\displaystyle\dfrac{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2+k-m})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-m-k+4})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m+k})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m-k+2})}{d_{1,k^{m}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By (
7.57a
) and (
7.57b
), there is an ambiguity when
m=2
.
In the case of
m=2
, the surjective morphism (
3.13b
)

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-n+k-1}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n-k+1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)

implies

\dfrac{d_{1,n}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-n+k-1}z)d_{1,n}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{n-k+1}z)}{d_{1,k^{2}}(z)}=\dfrac{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k+4})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k+2})}{d_{1,k^{2}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}],

resolving the ambiguity.
Now we consider
m\geq 3
, and by the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m+1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}},

our insertion follows since we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{1,k^{m}}(z)(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m+2+k})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{4n+m-k+4})}{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{m+k})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+m-k+2})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].\qed

As Proposition
7.16
, we can obtain the following proposition.

Proposition 7.46
.

For any
m\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 2}
and
1\leq k,l<n
, we have

(7.58)

\displaystyle d_{l,k^{m}}(z)=\prod_{s=1}^{\min(k,l)}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{|k-l|+m-1+2s})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k-l+m+1+2s})

As Lemma
7.6
and Lemma
7.17
, we have the following analogous statement.

Lemma 7.47
.

For
1\leq k,l\leq n-1
and
\max(m,p)>1
, we have

d_{l^{p},k^{m}}(z)
divides
\prod_{t=0}^{\min(m,p)-1}\prod_{s=1}^{\min(k,l)}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{|k-l|+\left\lvert m-p\right\rvert+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2-k-l+\left\lvert m-p\right\rvert+2s+2t})
.

Proposition 7.48
.

For any
m,p\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 1}
with
\max(m,p)\geq 2
,
1\leq k,l<n
and

\left\lvert m-p\right\rvert\geq\mu\mathbin{:=}\max(n-\max(k,l),\min(k,l)-1),

we have

d_{l^{p},k^{m}}(z)=\prod_{t=0}^{\min(m,p)-1}\prod_{s=1}^{\min(k,l)}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{|k-l|+\left\lvert m-p\right\rvert+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2-k-l+\left\lvert m-p\right\rvert+2s+2t}).

Proposition 7.49

(Theorem
6.5
for
\max(l,k)<n
, and
m\equiv_{2}0
or
p\equiv_{2}0
)
.

For
k,l<n
and
m,p\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 1}
, we have

d_{k^{2m},l^{p}}(z)=\prod_{t=0}^{\min(2m,p)-1}\prod_{s=1}^{\min(k,l)}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{|k-l|+|2m-p|+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2-k-l+|2m-p|+2s+2t})

Proof of
Theorem
6.5
for
\max(l,k)<n
, and
m\equiv_{2}0
or
p\equiv_{2}0

in type
C_{n}^{(1)}
.

Recall that we have a homomorphism in (
5.10d
)

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-1)^{-n-m+k}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{-1-n+k}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-1)^{n+m-k}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-k}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{2m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

which yields the homomorphism

\displaystyle\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{2m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-1)^{-n-m+k}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1+k}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-1)^{n+m-k}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-k}}

by duality. Then we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m}}(z)d_{l^{p},n^{m}}((-1)^{-n-m+k}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1+k}z)}{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}((-1)^{n+m-k}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-k}z)}\times\dfrac{a_{l^{p},n^{m}}((-1)^{n+m-k}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-k}z)}{a_{l^{p},k^{2m}}(z)a_{l^{p},n^{m}}((-1)^{-n-m+k}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1+k}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

We assume that
l\leq k
and
p\leq 2m
. By a direct calculation, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{l^{p},n^{m}}((-1)^{n+m-k}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-k}z)}{a_{l^{p},k^{2m}}(z)a_{l^{p},n^{m}}((-1)^{-n-m+k}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1+k}z)}=\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}\dfrac{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{l-k-2m+p-2s-2t})}{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-l-k+2m-p+2s+2t})}

and

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m}}(z)d_{l^{p},n^{m}}((-1)^{-n-m+k}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1+k}z)}{d_{l^{p},n^{m}}((-1)^{n+m-k}q_{\mathfrak{s}}^{n+1-k}z)}=\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-l-k+2m-p+2s+2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-l+k+2m-p+2s+2t})}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-l-k+2m-p+2s+2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-l+k+2m-p+2s+2t})}\times\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}\dfrac{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{l-k-2m+p-2s-2t})}{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-l-k+2m-p+2s+2t})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{l-k-2m+p-2s-2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-l+k+2m-p+2s+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}]

(7.59)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m}}(z)}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-l+k+2m-p+2s+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Then our assertion for this case follows from Lemma
7.47
.
The remaining cases can be proved in a similar way.
∎

Lemma 7.50
.

For
\mathfrak{g}=C_{n}^{(1)}
,
k,l<n
,
we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2-|k-l|+|m-p|+2t}})

\displaystyle\hskip 30.1388pt=\begin{cases}\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2-|k-l|+|m-p|+1+2t}})&\text{ if }m\geq p,\\
\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2-|k-l|+|m-p|+2t}})&\text{ if }m<p,\end{cases}

for
0\leq t\leq\min(m,p)-1
.

Proof.

We set
\max(k,l)=a
and
\min(k,l)=b
.

(i) We first assume
m\geq p
.
Note that

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+3-a+b+m-p+2t}}\simeq\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+3-a+b+2m-p+2t}}\mathbin{\scalebox{.9}{$\nabla$}}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2-a+b+m-p+2t}}

and

\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+3-a+b+2m-p+2t}})=\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n-2}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+3-a+b+2m-p+2t}})=0

by

d_{k,l^{p}}(z)=\prod_{s=1}^{b}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a-b+p-1+2s})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-a-b+p+1+2s}).

Namely,

(a)

2n+3-a+b+2m-p+2t\neq a-b+p-1+2s\iff(n-a)+2+(m-p)+(b-s)>0\geq-t
,

(b)

2n+3-a+b+2m-p+2t\neq 2n-a-b+p+1+2s\iff 2+(m-p)+(b-s)>0\geq-t
,

(c)

4n+5-a+b+2m-p+2t\neq a-b+p-1+2s\iff n+3+(n-a)+(m-p)+(b-s)>0\geq-t
,

(d)

4n+5-a+b+2m-p+2t\neq 2n-a-b+p+1+2s\iff n+2+(m-p)+(b-s)>0\geq-t
,

for
1\leq s\leq b
and
0\leq t\leq p-1
.
Thus we have the assertion by Lemma
2.12
(
ii
).

(ii) Now let us assume that
m<p
.
Note that

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}}\simeq\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\mathbin{\scalebox{.9}{$\nabla$}}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1-p}}.

and

\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2-a+b+p-m+2t}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1-p}})=\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{4n+4-a+b+p-m+2t}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1-p}})=0

by

d_{l,k^{m}}(z)=\prod_{s=1}^{\min(k,l)}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a-b+m-1+2s})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-a-b+m+1+2s}).

Namely,

(a)

2n+3-a+b+2p-m+2t\neq a-b+m-1+2s\iff(n-a)+2+(p-m)+(b-s)>0\geq-t
,

(b)

2n+3-a+b+2p-m+2t\neq 2n-a-b+m+1+2s\iff 2+(p-m)+(b-s)>0\geq-t
,

(c)

4n+5-a+b+2p-m+2t\neq a-b+m-1+2s\iff n+3+(n-a)+(p-m)+(b-s)>0\geq-t
,

(d)

4n+5-a+b+2p-m+2t\neq 2n-a-b+m+1+2s\iff n+2+(p-m)+(b-s)>0\geq-t
,

for
1\leq s\leq b
and
0\leq t\leq m-1
. Thus we have the assertion by Lemma
2.12
(
i
).
∎

Lemma 7.51
.

For
\mathfrak{g}=C_{n}^{(1)}
,
k,l<n
and
m,p\in 2\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 1}+1

we assume that

\min(k,l)=1.

Then

(1)

we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{|k-l|+|m-p|+2}})

\displaystyle\hskip 30.1388pt=\begin{cases}\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{|k-l|+p-m+3}})&\text{ if }m<p,\\
\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{|k-l|+m-p+2}})&\text{ if }m\geq p,\end{cases}

(2)

we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2+|k-l|+|m-p|+2}})

\displaystyle\hskip 30.1388pt=\begin{cases}\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2+|k-l|+p-m+3}})&\text{ if }m<p,\\
\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2+|k-l|+m-p+2}})&\text{ if }m\geq p.\end{cases}

Proof.

We set
a\mathbin{:=}\max(k,l)
.

(1-i) We first assume
m<p
.
Note that

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+p-m+2}}\simeq\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+p+2}}\mathbin{\scalebox{.9}{$\nabla$}}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+p-m+1}}

and

\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+p+2}})=\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n-2}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+p+2}})=0

by

d_{k,l^{p}}(z)=(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+p})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-a+p+2}).

Namely,

\displaystyle a+p+2\neq a+p,

\displaystyle a+p+2\neq 2n-a+p+2\iff n-a\neq 0,

\displaystyle 2n+2+a+p+2\neq a+p,

\displaystyle 2n+a+p+4\neq 2n+2-a+p\iff a+1\neq 0.

Thus we have the assertion by Lemma
2.12
(
ii
);

(1-ii) Now let us assume that
m\geq p
.
Note that

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}}\simeq\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\mathbin{\scalebox{.9}{$\nabla$}}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1-p}}.

Since

\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+m-p+1}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1-p}})=\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+a+m-p+3}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1-p}})=0

d_{l,k^{m}}(z)=(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+m})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-a+m+2s}),

as in the previous case, we have the assertion by Lemma
2.12
(
i
).

(2-i) We first assume
m<p
.
Note that

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+p-m+2n+4}}\simeq\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+p+2n+4}}\mathbin{\scalebox{.9}{$\nabla$}}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+p-m+2n+3}}

and

\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+4+a+p}})=\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n-2}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+p+2n+4}})=0

by

d_{k,l^{p}}(z)=(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+p})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-a+p+2}).

Namely,

\displaystyle a+p+2n+4\neq a+p,

\displaystyle a+p+2n+4\neq 2n+2-a+p\iff a+1\neq 0,

\displaystyle 4n+6+a+p\neq a+p,

\displaystyle 4n+6+a+p\neq 2n+2-a+p\iff n+a+2\neq 0.

Thus we have the assertion by Lemma
2.12
(
ii
);

(2-ii) Now let us assume that
m\geq p
.
Note that

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}}\simeq\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\mathbin{\scalebox{.9}{$\nabla$}}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1-p}}.

Since

\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+m-p+2n+3}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1-p}})=\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+a+m-p+2n+5}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1-p}})=0

d_{l,k^{m}}(z)=(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{a+m})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-a+m+2s}),

as in the previous case, we have the assertion by Lemma
2.12
(
i
).
∎

Proof of
Theorem
6.5
for
(i)

k\neq l<n
or
(ii)

\min(k,l)=1
in type
C_{n}^{(1)}
.

Since the case when
\min(p,m)=1
is already covered, let us assume
\min(p,m)>1
.
Also the case when
\min(k,l)=1
is covered by Proposition (
7.48
) and hence we shall assume
\min(k,l)>1
.
By the previous proof, it suffices to show that when
m,p
are odd. Thus we shall consider only
d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)
when
p
is odd.
Note that we have to prove that
d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)
coincides with

(7.60)

\displaystyle D_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)\mathbin{:=}\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-l+2m+1-p+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2-k-l+2m+1-p+2s+2t}).

We assume further that
k\geq l
and
2m+1\geq p
.

From fusion rule

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{2m+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2m-2}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{2m+2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}},

we obtain

\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)d_{l^{p},k}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2m-2}z)}{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+2}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}z)}\times\dfrac{a_{l^{p},k^{2m+2}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}z)}{a_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)a_{l^{p},k}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2m-2}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By direct computation, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)d_{l^{p},k}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2m-2}z)}{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+2}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}z)}

\displaystyle\hskip 43.05542pt=\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-l+p+2m+1+2s})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+3-k-l+p+2m+2s+2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-l+2m+3-p+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k-l+2m+5-p+2s+2t})}

\displaystyle\hskip 43.05542pt=\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=1}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-l+2m+1-p+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k-l+2m+3-p+2s+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}],

by applying the denominator formulas
d_{l^{p},k^{2m+2}}(z)
and
d_{l,k^{2m+1}}(z)
.
Hence Lemma
7.47
implies that

(7.61)

\displaystyle d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)

\displaystyle=\prod_{t=1}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-l+2m-p+1+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k-l+2m-p+3+2s+2t})

\displaystyle\times\prod_{s=1}^{l}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-l+2m-p+1+2s})^{\epsilon_{s}}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k-l+2m-p+3+2s})^{\epsilon^{\prime}_{s}}

for
\epsilon_{s},\epsilon^{\prime}_{s}\in\{0,1\}
for
1\leq s\leq l

By Lemma
7.51
,

(7.62)

we have no ambiguity when
l=1
; i.e
\epsilon_{1}=\epsilon^{\prime}_{1}=1
.

From the homomorphism obtained from Theorem
5.12
(
5.10a
) with the restriction
\min(k,l)=1

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-l+3}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{(l-1)^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})},

we obtain

\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)d_{1^{p},k^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-l+3}z)}{d_{(l-1)^{p},k^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}\times\dfrac{a_{(l-1)^{p},k^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}{a_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)a_{1^{p},k^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-l+3}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By direct calculation, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{(l-1)^{p},k^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}{a_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)a_{1^{p},k^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-l+3}z)}=\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\dfrac{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{l-k-2m+p-3-2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n+k+l-2m+p-5-2t})}{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{l-k+2m-p+1+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n+k+l+2m-p-1+2t})}

and the induction on
l
from (
7.62
) implies

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)d_{1^{p},k^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-l+3}z)}{d_{(l-1)^{p},k^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n+k+l+2m-p-1+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-k+l+2m-p+1+2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-l+2m-p+1+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k-l+2m-p+3+2s+2t})}

Thus we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n+k+l+2m-p-1+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-k+l+2m-p+1+2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-l+2m-p+1+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k-l+2m-p+3+2s+2t})}

\displaystyle\hskip 64.58313pt\times\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\dfrac{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{l-k-2m+p-3-2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n+k+l-2m+p-5-2t})}{(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{l-k+2m-p+1+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n+k+l+2m-p-1+2t})}

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{l-k-2m+p-3-2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-2n+k+l-2m+p-5-2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-l+2m-p+1+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k-l+2m-p+3+2s+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}]

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-l+2m-p+1+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k-l+2m-p+3+2s+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Hence Lemma
7.47
implies that

(7.63)

\displaystyle d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)

\displaystyle=\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-l+2m-p+1+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k-l+2m-p+3+2s+2t})

\displaystyle\times\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k+l+2m-p+1+2t})^{\epsilon_{t}}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k+l+2m-p+3+2s+2t})^{\epsilon^{\prime}_{t}}

for
\epsilon_{t},\epsilon^{\prime}_{t}\in\{0,1\}
for
1\leq t\leq p-1

From (
7.63
), we need to show

(7.64)

\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k+l+2m-p+1+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2-k+l+2m+1-p+2t})
appears in
d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)
with the same multiplicities of
D_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)
in (
7.60
).

By Lemma
7.50
and Proposition
7.49
, (
7.64
) for
\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n+2-k+l+2m+1-p})
follows.
Namely, we have

(7.65)

\displaystyle d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)

\displaystyle=\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{l-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k-l+2m-p+1+2s+2t})(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k-l+2m-p+3+2s+2t})

\displaystyle\times\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k+l+2m-p+1+2t})^{\epsilon_{t}}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{2n-k+l+2m-p+3+2s+2t})

for
\epsilon_{t}\in\{0,1\}
for
0\leq t\leq p-1
.

Now we assume further
k>l
.
Note that
l<n-1
by the assumption and hence we have
n>k>l
.
Then the T-system
(p=2p^{\prime}+1)

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{2p^{\prime}+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{2p^{\prime}+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{(l+1)^{2p^{\prime}+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{(l-1)^{2p^{\prime}+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)

and (
7.65
) yield

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)d_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}z)}{d_{(l+1)^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)d_{(l-1)^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k+l+2m-p+2+2t})^{\epsilon_{t}}\times\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k+l+2m-p+2t})^{\epsilon_{t}}}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k+l+2m-p+2+2t})^{\epsilon_{t}}\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{k+l+2m-p+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}]

by induction hypothesis on
l
since

(7.66)

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{(l+1)^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)a_{(l-1)^{p},k^{2m+1}}(z)}{a_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})z)a_{l^{p},k^{2m+1}}((-q_{\mathfrak{s}})^{-1}z)}=1.

Then we can conclude that
\epsilon_{t}=1
for all
0\leq t\leq p-1
, as we desired.

The remaining cases can be proved in a similar way.
∎

Remark 7.52
.

(a)

In the proof of the above theorem, (
7.66
) does not hold when
k=l
. Thus it is not easy to remove the ambiguity.

(b)

As we can see in (
7.65
), we only do not know the multiplicities of
those roots of
d_{k^{p},k^{m}}(z)
for
m,p\in 2\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 1}+1
. Hence we can determine whether
\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{k^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{x}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{y}

is simple or not completely (see Section
A
below for comparison between the conjectural formulas and (
7.65
)).

(c)

In Section
A
, we discuss an auxiliary technique that resolves the ambiguities in multiplicities.

Corollary 7.53
.

For
m\leq n
, the KR module
\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{x}

(x\in\mathbf{k}^{\times})
is a root module.

7.4.
Proof for type
D^{(1)}_{n}

In this subsection, we first prove our assertion on
d_{{n^{\prime}}^{m},n^{p}}(z)
for
n^{\prime}\in\{n-1,n\}
and on
d_{k^{m},{n^{\prime}}^{p}}(z)
.
Then we will prove our assertion on
d_{k^{m},l^{p}}(z)
for
1\leq k,l<n-1
, by applying the similar argument in Section
7.1
.

7.4.1.
d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)
and
d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)

Here we will prove our assertion on
d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)
and
d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)
.
We assume that
n
is even for this case. When
n
is odd, we can prove in the similar way.
Note that
i^{*}=i
, and recall that

\displaystyle d_{n,n-1}(z)=\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{\lfloor\frac{n-1}{2}\rfloor}\big(z-(-q)^{4s}\big),\ \ d_{n,n}(z)=d_{n-1,n-1}(z)=\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor}\big(z-(-q)^{4s-2}\big),

\displaystyle a_{n,n-1}(z)\equiv\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n-1}[4s-2]}{[2n-2]\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n-2}[4s]},\ \ a_{n,n}(z)=a_{n-1,n-1}(z)\equiv\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=0}^{n-1}[4s]}{[2n-2]\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n-1}[4s-2]}.

Also, by Theorem
3.15
(
3.13
), we have the following:
For
1\leq l\leq n-2
and
n^{\prime},n^{\prime\prime}\in\{n,n-1\}
such that
n^{\prime}-n^{\prime\prime}\equiv n-l\pmod{2}
, there
exist surjective homomorphisms

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{\prime}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{-n+l+1}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{\prime\prime}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n-l-1}}\to\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\text{ and }\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{l}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{-n+l+1}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n-l-1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{l}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n-1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

which can be obtained from Theorem
3.15
and (
3.13c
), respectively.

Lemma 7.54
.

For
m\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 1}
, we have

d_{n,n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{s=1}^{\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-3})\quad\text{ and }\quad d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{s=1}^{\lfloor\frac{n-1}{2}\rfloor}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-1}).

Proof.

(a-1) For simplicity, write
n^{\prime}=\dfrac{n}{2}
.
By the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{1-m}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

we have

(7.67)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n,n^{m-1}}((-q)z)d_{n,n}((-q)^{1-m}z)}{d_{n,n^{m}}(z)}\equiv\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-5})(z-(-q)^{4s+m-3})}{d_{n,n^{m}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{m-1}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{-1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

we have

(7.68)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n,n}((-q)^{m-1}z)d_{n,n^{m-1}}((-q)^{-1}z)}{d_{n,n^{m}}(z)}\equiv\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s-m-1})(z-(-q)^{4s+m-3})}{d_{n,n^{m}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

On the other hand, the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{2n+m-3}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{-1}}

implies that

\dfrac{d_{n,n^{m}}(z)d_{n,n}((-q)^{-2n-m+3}z)}{d_{n,n^{m-1}}((-q)z)}\times\dfrac{a_{n,n^{m-1}}((-q)z)}{a_{n,n^{m}}(z)a_{n,n}((-q)^{-2n-m+3}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By our induction hypothesis and a direct computation, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{n,n^{m-1}}((-q)z)}{a_{n,n^{m}}(z)a_{n,n}((-q)^{-2n-m+3}z)}

\displaystyle=\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}\dfrac{(z-(-q)^{m-5+4s})}{(z-(-q)^{m-3+4s})},

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n,n^{m}}(z)d_{n,n}((-q)^{-2n-m+3}z)}{d_{n,n^{m-1}}((-q)z)}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{n,n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{2n+m-5+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-5})}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n,n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{2n+m-5+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-5})}\times\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}\dfrac{(z-(-q)^{m-5+4s})}{(z-(-q)^{m-3+4s})}=\dfrac{d_{n,n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{2n+m-5+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{m-3+4s})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}]

Thus we can conclude that

d_{n,n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-5})^{\epsilon_{s}^{\prime}}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-3})

for some
\epsilon_{s}^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}
.

(b-1)
For simplicity, write
n^{\prime\prime}=\left\lfloor\dfrac{n-1}{2}\right\rfloor
.
By the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{1-m}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

we have

(7.69)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n-1,n^{m-1}}((-q)z)d_{n-1,n}((-q)^{1-m}z)}{d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)}\equiv\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-3})(z-(-q)^{4s+m-1})}{d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{m-1}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{-1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

we have

(7.70)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n-1,n}((-q)^{m-1}z)d_{n-1,n^{m-1}}((-q)^{-1}z)}{d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)}\equiv\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s-m+1})(z-(-q)^{4s+m-1})}{d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

On the other hand, the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{2n+m-3}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{-1}}

implies that we have

\dfrac{d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)d_{n-1,n}((-q)^{-2n-m+3}z)}{d_{n^{m-1},n-1}((-q)z)}\times\dfrac{a_{(n^{m-1},n-1}((-q)z)}{a_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)a_{n-1,n}((-q)^{-2n-m+3}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By induction and a direct computation, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{n^{m-1},n-1}((-q)z)}{a_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)a_{n-1,n}((-q)^{-2n-m+3}z)}

\displaystyle=\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}\dfrac{(z-(-q)^{m-3+4s})}{(z-(-q)^{m-1+4s})},

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)d_{n-1,n}((-q)^{-2n-m+3}z)}{d_{(n-1)^{m-1},n}((-q)z)}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{2n+m-3+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-3})}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{2n+m-3+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-3})}\times\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}\dfrac{(z-(-q)^{m-3+4s})}{(z-(-q)^{m-1+4s})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{2n+m-3+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{m-1+4s})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Note that
2n+m-3+4s^{\prime}\neq m-1+4s
.
Thus we can conclude that

d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-3})^{\epsilon_{s}^{\prime\prime}}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-1})

for some
\epsilon_{s}^{\prime\prime}\in\{0,1\}
.

By (
7.67
), (
7.68
), (
7.69
) and (
7.70
),
it is enough to consider when
m\leq n
.

(a,b) Note that we have a surjective homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{-n+2}}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n-2}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n-1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

which implies

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{1,n^{m}}((-q)^{-n+2}z)d_{n-2,n^{m}}((-q)z)}{d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)d_{n,n^{m}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Note that

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{1,n^{m}}((-q)^{-n+2}z)d_{n-2,n^{m}}((-q)z)}{d_{n-1,n^{m}}(z)d_{n,n^{m}}(z)}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n-1}(z-(-q)^{m-1+2s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-3})^{\epsilon_{s}^{\prime\prime}}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-1})\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-5})^{\epsilon_{s}^{\prime}}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-3})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Thus we can conclude that
\epsilon_{s}^{\prime}=\epsilon_{s}^{\prime\prime}=0
for all cases. Hence our assertion follows.
∎

As Lemma
7.32
, we obtain the following lemma:

Lemma 7.55
.

For
m,p\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 1}
, we have

d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)\text{ divides }\prod_{t=0}^{\min(p,m)-1}\prod_{s=1}^{\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t-2+|p-m|}).

Lemma 7.56
.

For
|m-p|\geq n-2
, we have

d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{t=0}^{\min(p,m)-1}\prod_{s=1}^{\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t-2+|p-m|}).

Proof.

For simplicity, write
n^{\prime}=\dfrac{n}{2}
. Without loss of generality, we can assume that
2\leq p\leq m
.
Then our assertion can be re-written as follows:

d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t-2+m-p}).

By the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{2n+p-3}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{p-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{-1}}

tells that we have

\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{n,n^{m}}((-q)^{-2n-p+3}z)}{d_{n^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q)z)}\times\dfrac{a_{n^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q)z)}{a_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{n,n^{m}}((-q)^{-2n-p+3}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By induction, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{n,n^{m}}((-q)^{-2n-p+3}z)}{d_{n^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q)z)}=\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{2n+p+m-6+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-2}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t-2+m-p})}

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{n^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q)z)}{a_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{n,n^{m}}((-q)^{-2n-p+3}z)}=\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}\dfrac{(z-(-q)^{p-m-4+4s})}{(z-(-q)^{m+p-4+4s})}

Thus we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{2n+p+m-6+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-2}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t-2+m-p})}\times\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}\dfrac{(z-(-q)^{p-m-4+4s})}{(z-(-q)^{m+p-4+4s})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{2n+p+m-6+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{m-p-2+4s+2t})}\times\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{p-m-4+4s})\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Since
2n+p+m-6+4s^{\prime}\neq m-p-2+4s+2t
,
the above equation is equivalent to

(7.71)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{p-m-4+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{m-p-2+4s+2t})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Therefore,
our assertion holds, since
(u\mathbin{:=}m-p\geq n-2)

\displaystyle p-m-4+4s^{\prime}\neq m-p-2+4s+2t\iff 2(s^{\prime}-s)-t\neq u+1

for
1\leq s\leq n^{\prime}
and
0\leq t\leq p-1
.
The remaining cases can be proved in a similar way.
∎

Lemma 7.57
.

For
m,p\geq 1
,

d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)\text{ divides }\prod_{t=0}^{\min(p,m)-1}\prod_{s=1}^{\lfloor\frac{n-1}{2}\rfloor}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+|p-m|}).

Proof.

(1) We first assume that
2\leq p\leq m
.
By the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{(n-1)^{p-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)}\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n-1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{1-p}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{(n-1)^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),

we have

\dfrac{d_{(n-1)^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q)z)d_{n-1,n^{m}}((-q)^{1-p}z)}{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}]

since

\dfrac{a_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)}{a_{(n-1)^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q)z)a_{n-1,n^{m}}((-q)^{1-p}z)}=1.

By induction, we have

d_{(n-1)^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q)z)\text{ divides }\prod_{t=0}^{p-2}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m-p}),

and we know

d_{n-1,n^{m}}((-q)^{1-p}z)=\prod_{s=1}^{\lfloor\frac{n-1}{2}\rfloor}(z-(-q)^{4s+m+p-2}).

Thus our assertion follows.
∎

Lemma 7.58
.

For
|m-p|\geq n-2
, we have

d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{t=0}^{\min(p,m)-1}\prod_{s=1}^{\lfloor\frac{n-1}{2}\rfloor}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+|p-m|}).

Proof.

For simplicity, write
n^{\prime\prime}=\lfloor\frac{n-1}{2}\rfloor
.

(1) We first assume that
2\leq p\leq m
. Then our assertion can be re-written as follows:

d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)=\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m-p}).

By the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{(n-1)^{p}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n-1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{2n+p-3}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{(n-1)^{p-1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{-1}}

tells that we have

\dfrac{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{(n-1),n^{m}}((-q)^{-2n-p+3}z)}{d_{(n-1)^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q)z)}\times\dfrac{a_{(n-1)^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q)z)}{a_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{(n-1),n^{m}}((-q)^{-2n-p+3}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By induction, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{(n-1),n^{m}}((-q)^{-2n-p+3}z)}{d_{n-1^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q)z)}=\dfrac{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{2n+p+m-4+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-2}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m-p})}.

A direct computation shows

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{(n-1)^{p-1},n^{m}}((-q)z)}{a_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{(n-1),n^{m}}((-q)^{-2n-p+3}z)}=\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}\dfrac{(z-(-q)^{-m+p-2+4s})}{(z-(-q)^{m+p-2+4s})}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{2n+p+m-4+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-2}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m-p})}\times\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}\dfrac{(z-(-q)^{-m+p-2+4s})}{(z-(-q)^{m+p-2+4s})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{2n+p+m-4+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m-p})}\times\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{-m+p-2+4s})\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Note that

\displaystyle 2n+p+m-4+4s^{\prime}\neq 4s+2t+m-p\iff n+p-2\neq 2(s-s^{\prime})+t

for
1\leq s,s^{\prime}\leq n^{\prime\prime}
and
0\leq t\leq p-1
. Thus the above equation can be written as

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)\times\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{-m+p-2+4s})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m-p})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

Since
(u\mathbin{:=}m-p\geq n-2)

\displaystyle-m+p-2+4s^{\prime}\neq 4s+2t+m-p\iff 2(s^{\prime}-s)-t\neq u+1

for
1\leq s,s^{\prime}\leq n^{\prime\prime}
and
0\leq t\leq p-1
, the assertion follows with Lemma
7.57
. The remaining case can be proved in a similar way.
∎

Proof of
Theorem
6.5
for
d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)
and
d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)
in type
D_{n}^{(1)}
.

Consider the homomorphism

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{-m-1}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m+1}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{-1}}

then we have

\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{n^{p},n}((-q)^{-m-1}z)}{d_{n^{p},n^{m+1}}((-q)^{-1}z)}\times\dfrac{a_{n^{p},n^{m+1}}((-q)^{-1}z)}{a_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{n^{p},n}((-q)^{-m-1}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By direct computation, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{n^{p},n^{m+1}}((-q)^{-1}z)}{a_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{n^{p},n}((-q)^{-m-1}z)}

\displaystyle=1

and

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{n^{p},n}((-q)^{-m-1}z)}{d_{n^{p},n^{m+1}}((-q)^{-1}z)}=\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m+p-2})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m-p})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=1}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m-p-2})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}]

which follows from the descending induction on
|m-p|
. Thus we can conclude that

d_{n^{p},n^{m}}=\prod_{t=1}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m-p-2})\times\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-p-2})^{\epsilon_{s}}

for some
\epsilon_{s}\in\{0,1\}
.

Similarly, we have

\dfrac{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{(n-1)^{p},n}((-q)^{-m-1}z)}{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m+1}}((-q)^{-1}z)}\times\dfrac{a_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m+1}}((-q)^{-1}z)}{a_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{(n-1)^{p},n}((-q)^{-m-1}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By direct computation, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m+1}}((-q)^{-1}z)}{a_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{(n-1)^{p},n}((-q)^{-m-1}z)}

\displaystyle=1

and

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{(n-1)^{p},n}((-q)^{-m-1}z)}{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m+1}}((-q)^{-1}z)}=\dfrac{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+p+m})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m+2-p})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}(z)}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=1}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m-p})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}]

which follows from the descending induction on
|m-p|
. Thus we can conclude that

d_{(n-1)^{p},n^{m}}=\prod_{t=1}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+2t+m-p})\times\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-p})^{\epsilon^{\prime}_{s}}

for some
\epsilon^{\prime}_{s}\in\{0,1\}
.

By the homomorphism obtained from Theorem
5.12
(
5.10b
) with the restriction
\min(k,l)=1

\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{(n-1)^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)\otimes\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n}}\twoheadrightarrow\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{(n-2)^{m}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)},

we have

\dfrac{d_{n^{p},(n-1)^{m}}(z)d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{n^{p},1^{m}}((-q)^{n}z)}{d_{n^{p},(n-2)^{m}}((-q)z)}\times\dfrac{a_{n^{p},(n-2)^{m}}((-q)z)}{a_{n^{p},(n-1)^{m}}(z)a_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{n^{p},1^{m}}((-q)^{n}z)}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}].

By direct computation, we have
(n=2n^{\prime})

\displaystyle\dfrac{a_{n^{p},(n-2)^{m}}((-q)z)}{a_{n^{p},(n-1)^{m}}(z)a_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)a_{n^{p},1^{m}}((-q)^{n}z)}=\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\dfrac{(z-(-q)^{p-m-2-2t})}{(z-(-q)^{m-p+2t})}

and

\displaystyle\dfrac{d_{n^{p},(n-1)^{m}}(z)d_{n^{p},n^{m}}(z)d_{n^{p},1^{m}}((-q)^{n}z)}{d_{n^{p},(n-2)^{m}}((-q)z)}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-p})^{\epsilon^{\prime}_{s}}\times\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-p-2})^{\epsilon_{s}}\times\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q)^{m-p+2t})}{\displaystyle\prod_{t=1}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n-2}(z-(-q)^{m-p+2(s+t)})\times\prod_{s=1}^{n-2}(z-(-q)^{m-p+2s})}

\displaystyle\hskip 64.58313pt\times\prod_{t=1}^{p-1}\prod_{s=1}^{n-1}(z-(-q)^{2s+2t+m-p})\times\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}\dfrac{(z-(-q)^{p-m-2-2t})}{(z-(-q)^{m-p+2t})}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-p})^{\epsilon^{\prime}_{s}}\times\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-p-2})^{\epsilon_{s}}}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n-2}(z-(-q)^{m-p+2s})}

\displaystyle\hskip 64.58313pt\times\prod_{t=1}^{p-1}(z-(-q)^{2n-2+2t+m-p})\times\prod_{t=0}^{p-1}(z-(-q)^{p-m-2-2t})\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}]

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\dfrac{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-p})^{\epsilon^{\prime}_{s}}\times\prod_{s=1}^{n^{\prime}}(z-(-q)^{4s+m-p-2})^{\epsilon_{s}}}{\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{n-2}(z-(-q)^{m-p+2s})}\in\mathbf{k}[z^{\pm 1}],

which implies
\epsilon_{s}=\epsilon^{\prime}_{s}=1
for
1\leq s\leq n^{\prime}-1
. Hence,
\epsilon_{n^{\prime}}=1
by the universal coefficient formula.
The remaining cases can be proved in a similar way.
∎

7.4.2.
d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)
for
1\leq l<n-1

We recall that by the diagram symmetry, we have
d_{l^{p},n^{m}}(z)=d_{l^{p},(n-1)^{m}}(z)
.
For
1\leq l<n-1
, we have

(7.72)

\displaystyle d_{k,n-1}(z)=d_{k,n}(z)=\displaystyle\prod_{s=1}^{k}\big(z-(-q)^{n-k-1+2s}\big),

\displaystyle a_{k,n-1}(z)=a_{k,n}(z)\equiv\dfrac{[n-k-1][3n+k-3]}{[n+k-1][3n-k-3]}.

Lemma 7.59
.

We have

d_{1,n^{2}}(z)=d_{1,(n-1)^{2}}(z)=(z-(-q)^{n+1}).

Proof.

By (
7.72
), we have
d_{1,n}(z)=z-(-q)^{n}
. Hence it is enough to consider

\displaystyle{\rm(i)}

\displaystyle\ \ \mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n-1}})=\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n}}\mathbin{\scalebox{.9}{$\nabla$}}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n-2}}),

\displaystyle{\rm(ii)}

\displaystyle\ \ \mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n+1}})=\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n+2}}\mathbin{\scalebox{.9}{$\nabla$}}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n}}).

By Theorem
3.12
,
\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n-1}})=0
.
Note that the sequences

(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n-2}})\quad\text{{and}}\quad(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n+2}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n}},\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big))

are normal, since

\mathfrak{d}(\mathscr{D}\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n-2}})=0\quad\text{{and}}\quad\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n-2}})=0,

respectively. Thus Lemma
2.17
(
b
)(v) tells that

\mathfrak{d}(\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{1}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big),\mathtt{V}\big(\hskip 0.86108pt\scalebox{.8}{\boxed{n^{2}}}\hskip 0.86108pt\big)_{(-q)^{n+1}})=1,

which completes the assertion.
∎

Lemma 7.60
.

For
m\geq 3
, we have

d_{1,n^{m}}(z)=(z-(-q)^{n+m-1})\quad\text{ and }\quad d_{1,(n-1)^{m}}(z)=(z-(-q)^{n+m-1}).

Proof.

By the homomorphism
